## Andon GATE53-MATRIX-BUDGETED-COMPILED-PREFLIGHT

Symptom:
All four post-Gate52 matrix subagents stopped before model execution because
the matrix command template requested budgeted compiled output with
`--target-output-kb 100` but omitted the runner-required
`--section-expansion-rounds`.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --case-name <matrix-case> --raw-input-path <prompt-or-raw-input> --run-dir <run-dir> --model gpt-5.5 --release-output-mode compiled --target-output-kb 100
```

Representative failure:

```text
staged current-skill harness: BLOCKED: budgeted compiled output requires --section-expansion-rounds >= 1 when --target-output-kb > 0 so selected sections can be expanded before strict assembly validates section budgets
```

Owner file / owner surface:
Coordinator matrix-launch command template and audit runbook surface. The
strict preflight is already owned by `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`
and was intentionally added in Gate 34.

5 Whys:

1. The matrix did not reach Stage 01 because each command omitted
   `--section-expansion-rounds` while requesting `--target-output-kb 100`.
2. The omission happened because the coordinator reused the matrix command
   shape after Gate 52 without carrying forward the Gate 34 budgeted-compiled
   preflight contract.
3. The launch prompt verified cwd, harness, prompt path, and run-dir absence,
   but did not verify the complete budgeted-output invocation contract.
4. This escaped Smoke A because Smoke A checked the runner and docs, not a
   dry-run of the exact four matrix commands.
5. Recurrence is prevented by requiring every budgeted compiled matrix command
   to include bounded expansion, normally `--section-expansion-rounds 2`, and
   treating missing expansion capacity as a shared-infrastructure launch Andon,
   not as case evidence.

Hansei:
The matrix coordinator must bind the whole command contract, not only the cwd
and paths. A pre-model harness block is useful proof that the runner protected
section-budget integrity, but it is not a consumed semantic smoke and must not
be interpreted as Trinitarian, TST, Khaybar, or Secularism behavior.

Countermeasure:
Refresh the matrix from scratch with new run directories. Every assigned
budgeted compiled smoke command must include:

```powershell
--release-output-mode compiled --target-output-kb 100 --section-expansion-rounds 2 --transport-retry-rounds 2
```

Smoke B command/result:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test
```

Result before launch: PASS as part of Gate 52. This Andon is a command-template
failure, so the next proof is the refreshed launch using the corrected command
shape.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed by non-release docs commit
`890d5859a074da62dc578b95e6e36fc2ac38cb97`; GitHub CI `26986869533` PASS and
Pages `26986869001` PASS. The refreshed matrix launched with bounded expansion.
The invalidated launch produced no case-specific Stage 07 evidence and opened
no Stage 8, retained promotion, package, tag, GitHub Release, release asset,
public sample publication, or provenance gate.

## Andon GATE54-TRINITARIAN-LAYERB-OPERATION-MASS

Symptom:
The Trinitarian run produced a 147937-byte `output.md`, reached the verifier
path, then failed before sidecars because Layer B submoves were
conclusion-shaped rather than operation-shaped.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\check_manual_smoke_render_contract.py --outputs .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260604-200126-matrix-trinitarian-j173\output.md
```

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage 07 public Layer B owner/TTP body
scaffold and manual-render contract interface. The checker remains strict.

5 Whys:

1. The checker failed because `do-attribute-precision` and `M8` submoves did
   not perform the owner-specific operation mass required before `Land(...)`.
2. Those submoves stayed conclusion-shaped because the Stage 07 public body
   scaffold allowed owner/TTP labels plus summary conclusions to stand in for
   person/nature, attribute-precision, and consequence-trace work.
3. The output could reach the verifier path because structural assembly,
   handoff, and witness surfaces were present even though high-mass public ACT
   body content remained under-executed.
4. Existing no-model canaries caught many owner-operation cases but did not
   force this Trinitarian high-mass public body shape to execute its selected
   owner operations before Land.
5. Recurrence is prevented by generic owner-operation body canaries and
   scaffold rules that require selected owners to emit operation-shaped
   target-operation-result evidence before any burden land claim.

Hansei:
Long public output and correct headings are not enough. The public ACT body
must be reconstructible as selected owner work, not as a polished conclusion.

Countermeasure:
Proposed next generic lane: harden Stage 07 owner-operation body scaffolds and
no-model canaries for high-mass selected owners, including
`do-attribute-precision` and `M8.consequence-trace`.

Smoke B command/result:
No countermeasure applied yet. Gemba rerun reproduced the failure with the
command above.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Open. Blocks Trinitarian Stage 8 sidecars, retained promotion, release matrix,
and release.

## Andon GATE54-TST-M8-DELTA-MRP-PARITY

Symptom:
The TST run produced a 170883-byte `output.md`, reached the verifier path, then
failed because ACT `²B₂` did not carry concrete M8 burden-local operation mass
and the witness named non-visible MRP source `B5`.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\check_mrp_generated_burden.py --outputs .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260604-200126-matrix-tst-lillard\output.md --show-advisories
```

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage 07 ACT/MRP public block and
field-witness/formal-reread scaffold. `tools/check_mrp_generated_burden.py`
remains strict.

5 Whys:

1. The checker failed because `²B₂` did not name a concrete burden-local delta
   and did not execute routed owner `M8`.
2. It also failed because `formal_reread_states` and `mrp_resultants` included
   `B5` without a matching visible MRP block.
3. These gaps existed because the model could fill owner labels and witness
   rows without deriving both the public operation body and the formal mirror
   from the same visible MRP source set.
4. Prior canaries predicted the M8 weak-delta and public/witness parity
   families but did not force this exact TST generated/witness shape before the
   live smoke.
5. Recurrence is prevented by pairing owner-operation mass canaries with a
   formal rule that every formal reread/MRP resultant source must be visible in
   the public MRP blocks or omitted from the mirror.

Hansei:
Witness rows cannot launder a missing public MRP block, and an owner code is
not owner execution.

Countermeasure:
Proposed next generic lane: require `M8.consequence-trace` to carry concrete
burden-local state change and public/witness MRP source parity before verifier
handoff.

Smoke B command/result:
No countermeasure applied yet. Gemba rerun reproduced the failure with the
command above.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Open. Blocks TST Stage 8 sidecars, retained promotion, release matrix, and
release.

## Andon GATE54-SECULARISM-M8-REGISTERED-OPERATION

Symptom:
The Secularism run produced a 133356-byte `output.md`, reached the verifier
path, then failed because ACT `⁴B₁` used operation token `dependency-exposure`
for `M8-reductio`, whose registered operation is `consequence-trace`.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\check_mrp_generated_burden.py --outputs .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260604-200126-matrix-secularism\output.md --show-advisories
```

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage 07 ACT operation-token scaffold
and owner-operation vocabulary alignment. `tools/check_mrp_generated_burden.py`
remains strict.

5 Whys:

1. The checker failed because the ACT row used an unregistered M8 operation
   token.
2. The token likely described the result/pressure shape, not the callable owner
   operation.
3. The scaffold did not sufficiently separate callable operation names from
   delta/result prose.
4. Existing operation-vocabulary canaries predicted this class but the model
   still crossed the operation/result boundary in the live output.
5. Recurrence is prevented by requiring ACT operation tokens to be selected
   from the registered owner operation floor while moving result labels such as
   dependency exposure into delta/result fields.

Hansei:
Availability of an owner is not activation unless the callable operation token
and the body evidence both match the owner contract.

Countermeasure:
Proposed next generic lane: add/strengthen M8 registered-operation canaries and
Stage 07 scaffold wording so `dependency-exposure` cannot replace
`M8-reductio.consequence-trace`.

Smoke B command/result:
No countermeasure applied yet. Gemba rerun reproduced the failure with the
command above.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Open. Blocks Secularism Stage 8 sidecars, retained promotion, release matrix,
and release.

## Gate54 Decision

The refreshed matrix is informative. The failures are case-specific outputs,
but three of four share the same owner-operation execution surface and the TST
failure also exposes public MRP / formal reread parity drift. Do not run another
model matrix yet. Open one generic no-model Stage 07 owner-operation mass and
public/witness parity repair lane before spending another model smoke.

Required next lane:

- Add/extend no-model fixtures for registered-operation alignment:
  `M8-reductio.consequence-trace` versus invalid `dependency-exposure`.
- Add owner-specific body fixtures for M8, V10, and DO-family
  attribute-precision work.
- Add public/witness MRP parity fixtures where `formal_reread_states` names a
  non-visible source.
- Patch the smallest Stage 07 runner/scaffold owner surface so selected owners
  emit operation-shaped target-operation-result evidence before Land.
- Do not weaken validators.
- Do not run model smokes, Stage 8 sidecars, retained promotion, package, tag,
  release assets, or provenance until the no-model repair passes Smoke B,
  safety scans, commit/push, and CI/Pages.

Non-claims:

- No case passed retained promotion.
- No case produced proof sidecars.
- No docs Output Grapher smoke ran.
- No release gate opened.

## Gate55 No-Model Countermeasure

this ledger entry.

- `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`
- `tests/mrp-generated-burden/invalid/`

Countermeasure:
The Stage 07 ACT section scaffold now explicitly distinguishes callable
operation tokens from result/pressure labels and adds owner-family operation
body requirements for:

- `M8`: registered operation token `consequence-trace`; trace concrete
  downstream implication/entailment and put result labels in
  `Result/state-change:`.
- `V10`: visibly vet transmission/provenance, content, and authority/status
  for the exact source pressure.
- `do-attribute-precision`: type person/nature or attribute relation, separate
  predication levels, and name the category confusion/transfer before Land.

- `tests/mrp-generated-burden/invalid/m8-unregistered-operation-token.md`

## Andon GATE56-TRINITARIAN-B5-PROJECTION-ELIGIBILITY

Symptom:
The Trinitarian run produced a 122136-byte Stage 07-valid and Grapher-readable
`output.md`, but Stage 08 B.5 projection was ineligible because generated `B5`
remained `carried-RECURSE`.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --case-name matrix-trinitarian-j173-gate55 --raw-input-path tests\smokes\v0.4.3.0-release-regression\prompts\01-trinitarian-j173.md --run-dir .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260604-210025-matrix-trinitarian-j173 --model gpt-5.5 --release-output-mode compiled --target-output-kb 100 --section-expansion-rounds 2 --transport-retry-rounds 2
```

`.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260604-210025-matrix-trinitarian-j173/proof-sidecars/matrix-trinitarian-j173-gate55.b5-full-ir-projection-eligibility.json`

Eligibility failure:

- `collapse_positive must be true before B.5 projection`
- `coverage_complete must be true before B.5 projection`
- `hold_partial_nodes must be empty before B.5 projection`

Owner file / owner surface:
Likely `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage 05 -> Stage 07
generated-B_MRP execution policy and release-regression proof objective.
The B.5 validator/eligibility boundary remains correct and strict.

5 Whys:

1. B.5 projection failed because `collapse_positive=false`,
   `coverage_complete=false`, and `hold_partial_nodes` contained `B5`.
2. Those fields were false/nonempty because generated `B5` was recorded as
   `carried-RECURSE`.
3. `B5` stayed carried because Stage 05 MRP discovered it after the Stage 04
   ACT execution plan had executed only B1-B4.
4. The pipeline correctly permits Stage 07-valid HOLD/PARTIAL/RECURSE output,
   but the release proof matrix requires collapse-positive retained-proof
   eligibility.
5. Recurrence is prevented by adding a no-model repair/eligibility policy that
   either routes generated B_MRP burdens into real ACT execution before B.5
   proof projection, or stops as explicitly non-retained/non-B.5 eligible
   without treating Stage 07 validity as release proof.

Hansei:
Stage 07 validity, public reconstruction, and docs/Grapher renderability are
not retained-proof eligibility. Honest carried generated burdens are allowed as
partial evidence, but they block v0.4.4.0 release proof until landed or
truthfully excluded from proof promotion.

Countermeasure:
Proposed, not yet applied: after read-only saturation/adversarial
classification, open a narrow no-model generated-B_MRP execution/eligibility
repair lane if the failure is in the producer/runner owner surface. Do not
weaken B.5 eligibility or promote HOLD/RECURSE output as retained proof.

Smoke B command/result:
No countermeasure has been applied yet. Direct Gemba checks above reproduced
the boundary: Stage 07 / graph checks PASS; B.5 projection eligibility FAILS
by strict sidecar.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Open. Blocks retained promotion, release-regression proof closure, package,
tag, GitHub Release, release assets, public sample publication, and provenance.

Gate56 decision:
Do not enter retained promotion or release. Per the matrix-loop rule, this
case-specific Andon does not invalidate the three other case reports, but it
does block v0.4.4.0 proof-matrix closure. Next gate is read-only saturation
classification followed by adversarial coverage-map falsification before any
new model-smoke discovery.

Non-claims:

- No retained promotion occurred for any Gate56 row.
- No package, tag, GitHub Release, release asset, public sample publication,
  or provenance opened.
- No Graphify/ActiveGraph proof claim is made.
- Output Grapher sidecars are docs/public visualization evidence only; the
  checker-owned sidecars remain proof authority.

## Gate58 Formalism Compliance Andon

Status:
Local Smoke B PASS; commit/push and CI/Pages monitoring pending. No further
model smoke, Stage 8, retained promotion, package, tag, GitHub Release, release
asset, public sample publication, or provenance opened in this lane.

Symptom:
Read-only formalism-compliance and adversarial formalism subagents found that
some checker/proof gates can still vary by path, fixture identity, or
prose/topic profile instead of only by DSL/IR proof objects. This is a
central-thesis release blocker because proof behavior must be defined over
selected/held `n_frame`, burden graph, owner family, callable operation,
source-status, route availability, controlled `delta_result`,
`B_LA`/`B_MRP`, generated-burden state, public MRP visibility,
`field_witness`/`formal_reread` parity, graph evidence, and proof-boundary
eligibility.

Failed check:
Read-only subagent audit, not a model smoke:

- Formalism-compliance audit: path/name/prose-gated findings in
  `tools/check_smoke_artifacts.py`,
  `tools/check_hard_output_scope_fulfillment.py`,
  `tools/check_manual_smoke_render_contract.py`,
  `tools/check_recursive_traversal_governance.py`,
  `tools/check_render_modes.py`, `tools/check_concealment_mode.py`, and
  `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`.
- Adversarial formalism audit: path-dependent strictness in
  `tools/check_field_witness_convergence.py` and
  `tools/check_graph_completeness.py`, retained-corpus sidecar trust in
  `tools/check_retained_proof_corpus.py`, prose-keyed register coverage, and
  generic execution-shaped TTP availability rows.

Owner file / owner surface:

- `tools/check_field_witness_convergence.py`
- `tools/check_graph_completeness.py`
- `tools/check_retained_proof_corpus.py`
- `tools/check_ttp_availability_canaries.py`
- follow-on owner surfaces for separately bounded lanes:
  `tools/check_smoke_artifacts.py`,
  `tools/check_hard_output_scope_fulfillment.py`,
  `tools/check_manual_smoke_render_contract.py`,
  `tools/check_recursive_traversal_governance.py`,
  `tools/check_render_modes.py`, `tools/check_concealment_mode.py`, and
  `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`

5 Whys:

1. The release proof surface is blocked because some gates can judge outputs by
   path, fixture identity, or prose/topic triggers rather than formal DSL/IR
   structure.
2. Those triggers survived because earlier de-hardcoding removed the most
   obvious case-name gates but left secondary strictness switches, retained
   sidecar trust, and prose-profile shortcuts.
3. The shortcuts existed because historic smokes and fixtures were named by
   domain cases, so checker intent was sometimes encoded near filenames or
   topic phrases instead of in `field_witness`, NAR, graph, source-status, and
   proof-boundary objects.
4. The gap was not fully caught earlier because CI validated the named fixture
   roots and retained manifests, but did not prove that neutral copies with
   identical IR receive the same checker result.
5. Recurrence is prevented by adding neutral/path-independent canaries and
   making retained proof gates recompute current public/witness/formal/graph
   agreement from retained outputs instead of trusting retained marker strings
   or fixture identity.

Hansei:
Removing case names is not enough. A checker can still remember a smoke through
path strictness, profile names, or loose topic lexicons. The correct standard is
formal: for any selected noetic structure, owner execution and proof eligibility
must be reconstructed from burden identity, owner/operation/delta/source-status
obligations, visible public evidence, witness parity, and graph evidence.

Countermeasure:
Implemented local no-model patch slice:

- make field-witness diagnostic completeness, generated-depth, normalized
  activation record, graph track, and no-new-resultant proof strictness depend
  on formal output structure rather than fixture root;
- require retained proof-corpus rows that claim B.1/B.2 to rerun current
  field-witness and graph validators over retained outputs;
- add neutral/name-independent canaries proving identical IR cannot pass or
  fail differently merely because of path/name;
- add a TTP availability negative canary for generic target/operation/result
  prose that names a selected owner without owner-specific operation semantics.
- keep projection-only B.5 canaries out of B.1/B.2 graph replay and narrow the
  canonical retained manifest's B.1/B.2/C.6 row claims to outputs that still
  satisfy the current validators.

Smoke A command/result:

```powershell
python tools\check_field_witness_convergence.py
python tools\check_graph_completeness.py
python tools\check_retained_proof_corpus.py
python tools\check_ttp_availability_canaries.py
```

Result:
PASS before mutation. The Andon is a false-negative coverage gap, not a
baseline failing fixture.

### Gate58 Smoke B Command-Selection Andon: Windows PyCompile Glob

Status:
Fixed locally.

Symptom:
The broad py_compile safety command failed on Windows with
`[Errno 22] Invalid argument: 'tools\\*.py'`.

Failed check:

```powershell
python -m py_compile tools\*.py
```

Owner file / owner surface:
Smoke B command surface; Windows native-command glob expansion behavior.

5 Whys:

1. Why did the command fail? Python received the literal `tools\*.py` path.
2. Why was it literal? Windows PowerShell does not expand the wildcard for this
   Python argument in the same way a POSIX shell does.
3. Why did I use that form? I copied the CI/Linux idiom instead of using the
   Windows-expanded form for the local shell.
4. Why was it not caught before execution? The focused py_compile command over
   the touched files had already passed, so I treated the broad command as a
   final sweep without rechecking shell semantics.
5. What prevents recurrence? Expand `tools/*.py` with PowerShell before passing
   file paths to `python -m py_compile`.

Hansei:
Command syntax is part of the proof surface. A failed local command must be
classified instead of silently replaced.

Countermeasure:
Rerun py_compile with PowerShell-expanded `tools/*.py` file paths.

Smoke B command/result:
PASS after countermeasure:

```powershell
$files = Get-ChildItem -Path tools -Filter *.py -File | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName }
python -m py_compile @files
```

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed locally. The broad py_compile sweep passes after shell-correct path
expansion.

Additional command-selection correction:
`git status --ignored=.matching --short ...` failed because `.matching` is not a
valid ignored mode. Rerun with
`git status --ignored=matching --short .daee\activegraph\20260604-gate58-formalism-hardening`,
which confirmed `.daee/` is ignored; `git ls-files` for the same ActiveGraph
path returned no tracked files.

### Gate58 Smoke B Andon: Overbroad NAR Required Predicate

Status:
Fixed locally inside the Gate58 no-model hardening lane.

Symptom:
Smoke B made `tools/check_graph_completeness.py` reject seven expected-valid
schema-light graph fixtures. The direct Gemba failure on
`tests/graph-completeness/valid/baseline-collapse-positive.md` was
`field_witness.normalized_activation_record missing`, which then caused
`witness_agreement` and `model_claim_consistency` graph conditions to fail.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\check_graph_completeness.py
```

Result:
FAIL. Expected-valid graph fixtures rejected:
`baseline-collapse-positive.md`, `curl-loopbreak-accounted.md`,
`divergence-generates-burden.md`, `generated-burden-collapse-positive.md`,
`restoration-after-field-witness.md`,
`restoration-recoil-authority-return.md`, and
`two-track-restoration-mrp.md`.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/check_field_witness_convergence.py`,
`normalized_record_required(...)`.

5 Whys:

1. Why did graph Smoke B fail? Because graph completeness now imports the
   stricter field-witness convergence result and the convergence checker
   demanded `normalized_activation_record` for schema-light graph fixtures.
2. Why did it demand NAR there? The first Gate58 patch made NAR required for
   any field_witness with `B_total` plus `owner_activations`.
3. Why was that too broad? Graph-completeness fixtures are valid B.1
   schema-light fixtures; they can prove graph/certificate conditions without
   claiming or carrying the full NAR projection surface.
4. Why did this escape the first patch design? The formal predicate was
   derived from retained proof-output expectations but accidentally applied to
   all owner-activation surfaces rather than to explicit NAR/projection claims.
5. What prevents recurrence? Require NAR strictly when it is present or
   explicitly claimed by the proof surface, and keep schema-light graph
   fixtures valid unless they opt into NAR/projection proof.

Hansei:
Formalism does not mean every checker-owned slice must carry every later proof
surface. It means each claimed surface must be strict over the DSL/IR object it
owns. B.1 graph fixtures must stay graph-strict, while retained/full proof rows
with NAR must stay NAR-strict.

Countermeasure:
Narrow `normalized_record_required(...)` so NAR is required by explicit NAR
presence/claim rather than by generic owner activation. Preserve strict NAR
parity whenever `normalized_activation_record` is present.

Smoke B command/result:
PASS after countermeasure:

```powershell
python tools\check_field_witness_convergence.py
python tools\check_graph_completeness.py
```

Result: field-witness PASS with `21` invalid fixtures including the neutral
path fixture; graph-completeness PASS with `34` invalid fixtures including the
neutral path fixture.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed locally. Graph fixtures pass again, and retained proof-corpus validation
still recomputes current field/graph validators for retained rows that claim
B.1/B.2.

### Gate58 Smoke B Andon: Retained Corpus Revalidation Blocker

Status:
Fixed locally inside the Gate58 no-model hardening lane.

Symptom:
After the Gate58 retained-corpus checker began recomputing current
field-witness and graph validators over retained outputs,
`tools/check_retained_proof_corpus.py` failed. The failure split into two
surfaces:

- a projection-only B.5 canary manifest
  (`valid/b5-proof-mode-target-strict-subset/manifest.json`) was forced
  through full public field/graph proof even though its `proof_boundary` says
  it is not a promoted retained proof artifact;
- the canonical sidecar-backed retained corpus includes B.1/B.2 row claims for
  retained outputs that now fail current graph/field conditions such as
  `two_track_mrp_closure` or `diagnostic_completeness`.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\check_retained_proof_corpus.py
```

Result:
FAIL. Representative failing artifacts:

- `tests/retained-proof-corpus/v0.4.3.0-schema-light/valid/b5-proof-mode-target-strict-subset/retained-proof-mode-output.md`
- `tests/retained-proof-corpus/v0.4.3.0-schema-light/valid/sidecar-backed/cases/exact-trinitarian-j173/output.md`
- `tests/retained-proof-corpus/v0.4.3.0-schema-light/valid/sidecar-backed/cases/dad8-science-only-c8/output.md`
- `tests/retained-proof-corpus/v0.4.3.0-schema-light/valid/sidecar-backed/cases/dad8-testimony-c8/output.md`

Owner file / owner surface:

- `tools/check_retained_proof_corpus.py`
- `tests/retained-proof-corpus/v0.4.3.0-schema-light/valid/sidecar-backed/manifest.json`

5 Whys:

1. Why did retained Smoke B fail? Because retained-corpus validation now
   recomputes current field/graph validators instead of trusting marker
   strings, and those validators found stale or narrower retained evidence.
2. Why were stale rows still expected-valid? The retained manifest records
   historical sidecar-backed proof rows, while current graph/field validators
   have since gained stricter B_MRP track and diagnostic-completeness
   obligations.
3. Why did projection-only B.5 fail? The first retained patch did not
   distinguish canary manifests that are explicitly not promoted retained proof
   artifacts from canonical sidecar-backed proof rows.
4. Why was this not caught earlier? The retained checker previously verified
   hashes, sidecar schema, Grapher markers, and limited output substrings, but
   did not replay the current validators for each retained output row.
5. What prevents recurrence? Retained validation must apply row-specific
   current validators only to rows that claim those proof gates, keep
   projection-only canaries in their own boundary, and force canonical retained
   manifests to be truthful about which cases still satisfy current B.1/B.2
   proof obligations.

Hansei:
Retained proof is not immortal proof. If validators harden, the manifest must
either recompute and pass under the current contract or truthfully narrow the
claim. Sidecar hashes preserve custody; they do not override current proof
eligibility.

Countermeasure:
Implemented. `check_retained_proof_corpus.py` now replays field/graph validators
only for retained rows that claim B.1/B.2, so projection-only B.5 canaries are
not forced through the wrong proof gate. The canonical sidecar-backed manifest
was narrowed so B.1/B.2 and C.6 targets list only cases that satisfy the
current validators. No new retained case was promoted.

Smoke B command/result:
PASS after countermeasure:

```powershell
python tools\check_retained_proof_corpus.py
```

Result: retained proof corpus check PASS; `2` valid manifests, `27` invalid
manifests, `14` coverage targets. Diagnostic Gemba command
`python tools\check_graph_completeness.py --outputs tests\retained-proof-corpus\v0.4.3.0-schema-light\valid\sidecar-backed\cases\dad8-testimony-c8\output.md`
still fails with `diagnostic_completeness`, `witness_agreement`, and
`model_claim_consistency`, and that artifact is now treated as negative
evidence rather than a current B.1/B.2 retained proof row.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed locally. No further model smoke, Stage 8, retained promotion, package,
tag, GitHub Release, release asset, public sample publication, or provenance
opened in this lane.

## Andon GATE59-TRINITARIAN-GENERATED-BMRP-TRACK

Status:
Fixed by Gate60 no-model countermeasure. The consumed Gate59 Trinitarian
artifact remains negative evidence and is not promoted; a fresh post-repair
matrix row is required before Stage 8 sidecars, retained promotion, or release.

Symptom:
Trinitarian produced `output.md` and passed most graph conditions, but Stage 07
graph completeness failed `two_track_mrp_closure` because generated `B6` had no
recognized `generated_burdens` track.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\check_graph_completeness.py --outputs .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260604-225444-matrix-trinitarian-j173\output.md --json
```

Result:
FAIL: `B_MRP tracks={'B6': ''}` and
`B_MRP burden B6 lacks generated_burdens track primary|restoration`.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage 06/07 generated-burden and
field_witness scaffold; `tools/check_graph_completeness.py` remains strict.

5 Whys:

1. Why did Trinitarian fail Stage 07? The generated MRP burden `B6` lacked a
   parseable two-track `generated_burdens.track`.
2. Why was the track missing? The producer preserved generated-held/open state
   and graph edge `B5->B6`, but did not require the field_witness/generated
   burden row to classify the generated burden into the graph track vocabulary.
3. Why did the system allow that? Prior canaries proved generated-held
   topology, provenance, and no false closure more than the exact
   two-track-certification field needed by graph completeness.
4. Why was it not caught before the model smoke? The no-model generated-MRP
   canaries covered B_MRP membership and dependency edges, but did not force
   the `primary|restoration` track field on every generated `G in B_MRP`.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Add a generic no-model canary and
   producer scaffold so every generated MRP burden `G` carries a parseable
   `generated_burdens.track` compatible with graph two-track closure, without
   converting open HOLD/RECURSE into collapse-positive proof.

Hansei:
Generated-burden openness and generated-burden track identity are separate
proof obligations. Preserving honest `carried-RECURSE` is necessary, but it is
not enough for graph reconstruction when the graph checker also requires
two-track placement.

Countermeasure:
Applied in `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`: generated MRP burden
records now derive a parser-stable `track` only from explicit or typed route
evidence (`track`, `restoration-recoil`, or `generated_burden_instantiation`),
not from case names, owner labels, titles, or prose. The Stage 07
field_witness scaffold now requires generated `G in B_MRP` to carry
`generation_depth`, `track`, and `generated_by`; the runner self-test requires
generated tracks to be canonical. `check_graph_completeness.py` remains strict.

Smoke B command/result:
PASS:

because visible ACT body_ref `⁴B₁` appeared more than once.

`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage 07 ACT partition/section
prompting and `tools/build_staged_governed_output.py` assembler partition
contract. The assembler remains strict.

5 Whys:

2. Why was `⁴B₁` duplicated? The model-authored Stage 07 ACT sections repeated
   a visible ACT body_ref within and across ACT partitions.
3. Why did the producer allow that? The section prompts and expansion flow
   still permit repeated visible ACT syntax when a section revisits the same
   burden for explanation or witness material.

   duplicate visible body refs at assembly time, but the producer scaffold did

   canary/scaffold so each ACT body_ref has exactly one visible execution body,
   and all later references use non-ACT cross-reference wording that cannot be
   parsed as a second ACT body.

The public ACT body_ref is an identity key, not a citation label. Reusing it
visibly makes the public body non-reconstructible even when the prose appears
coherent.

Countermeasure:

forbid repeating assigned `body_ref=` tokens in planning prose/examples and
expansion prompts forbid new `⟦ACT` rows or `body_ref=` tokens during expansion.
The assembler now rejects private planning/self-talk in public sections and has
a no-model invalid fixture for the observed leaked planning-text shape.

## Andon GATE59-SECULARISM-RAW-INPUT-SLASH-COMMAND

Status:
Fixed by Gate60 no-model countermeasure. The consumed Gate59 Secularism row
remains a no-output negative artifact; a fresh post-repair matrix row is required.

Symptom:
The Secularism subagent did not reach Stage 01. The runner interpreted
`/daee-epistemics refute secularism` as a path-like raw input that must resolve
inside the repository.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --case-name matrix-secularism-gate59 --raw-input "/daee-epistemics refute secularism" --run-dir .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260604-225444-matrix-secularism --model gpt-5.5 --release-output-mode compiled --target-output-kb 100 --section-expansion-rounds 2 --transport-retry-rounds 2
```

Result:
FAIL before Stage 01:
`staged current-skill harness: BLOCKED: Raw input must resolve inside repo root:
\daee-epistemics refute secularism`.

Owner file / owner surface:
Matrix command template and `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` raw input
preflight contract.

5 Whys:

1. Why did Secularism not run? The raw slash-command was treated as a path that
   failed repo-root resolution.
2. Why was it treated as a path? The `--raw-input` contract disambiguates
   path-like input conservatively, and a leading slash on Windows becomes a
   root-relative path.
3. Why did the coordinator use that shape? The matrix plan specified raw prompt
   text, and the launch template did not convert leading-slash prompts into an
   explicit input file or literal mode.
4. Why was it not caught before launch? Preflight checked prompt paths for the
   file-backed cases but did not dry-run the literal slash-command row.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Add a command-template canary and
   use a repo/ignored raw-input file or explicit literal-input handling for
   slash-command prompts.

Hansei:
Slash-command prompts need a literal-input contract. Shell quoting is not
enough when the runner's safety preflight interprets leading slashes as paths.

Countermeasure:
Applied in `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`: the runner now has an
explicit `--raw-input` literal-input mode, disables argparse abbreviation with
`allow_abbrev=False`, materializes literal input as ignored run-local
`raw-input.md`, and rejects simultaneous `--raw-input`/`--raw-input-path` use.
Resume runs cannot supply a fresh literal input.

Smoke B command/result:
PASS:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test --raw-input "/daee-epistemics refute secularism"
```

Failure is still preserved in
`.daee\release-regression-smokes\20260604-225444-matrix-secularism\runner.log`.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed for no-model command-contract hardening. No Stage 8/retained/release was
opened; fresh model evidence is still required.

## Gate61 Formal DSL/IR Hardening Closeout

Scope:
No model smokes, no Stage 8, no retained promotion, no package, no tag, no
GitHub Release, no release assets, no public sample publication, and no
provenance. This lane repaired post-Gate60 formalism risks found by the
read-only formalism/adversarial audits and by focused case-name/path searches.

Formal invariant:
Proof behavior must be keyed by selected/held `n_frame`, burden graph `DeltaB`,
owner family, callable owner operation, source-status, controlled
`delta_result`, generated-burden state, public MRP visibility,
field_witness/formal_reread parity, graph evidence, and proof-boundary
eligibility. Case names may remain in fixture names, retained IDs, prompts,
case-library prose, source-owned terminology, and audit evidence, but must not
drive pass/fail, routing, canonicalization, or release eligibility.

Countermeasures:

- `run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` no longer canonicalizes weak
  `delta_result` prose into accepted owner-local tokens; Stage 04 rows and
  field_witness deltas must use the controlled vocabulary directly.
- Generated-MRP `track` classification now uses explicit/typed route evidence
  only. Prose such as "restoration recoil" without typed route state is not
  proof of a restoration track.
- `diagnose.py` and `trigger-matrix.json` route `do-christian-extensions` from
  formal predication pressure (`feature.predication_confusion`), not the bare
  topic label.
- `check_level3_data_shapes.py` adds neutral route canaries proving bare topic
  labels remain inert while formal person/nature/predicate pressure routes.
- `check_render_modes.py` and `check_recursive_traversal_governance.py` replace
  Trinitarian-named M9 checks with route-generic predication/category/identity
  pressure and controlled state-change evidence.
- `check_smoke_artifacts.py` removes fixture-name contamination allowances and
  uses structural IR/manifest allowance families instead.
- `check_staged_runtime_handshake.py` requires generated-burden objects with
  `generated_by = MRP(Bn)`, parent pre-existence, exclusion from Stage 02
  `burden_floor`, and parent -> generated dependency edges.
- `check_field_witness_convergence.py` no longer infers register coverage from
  labels/titles/prose; nodes must carry explicit register fields.
- `check_ttp_availability_canaries.py` no longer lets `trinitarian` alone
  satisfy DO-family owner-body semantics; selected-owner evidence must name
  structural model/predication/person/nature/route/family/perfection/canon/
  authority operation content.

Formalism search classification:
Allowed hits remain in fixtures, case-library/source-owned terminology,
diagnostic term extraction, retained/audit IDs, and source-owned controlled
delta vocabulary such as `trinitarian-model-identified`. Forbidden proof
behavior found in this pass was the DO-family owner-body regex accepting a
topic label as execution evidence; it was patched as above.

### Andon GATE62C-TRINITARIAN-STAGE04-M9-DELTA-VOCABULARY

Symptom:
The Trinitarian smoke passed Stage 01-03, then Stage 04 failed after the
model produced ACT rows. The third ACT row used an M9 delta token not in the
source-owned controlled vocabulary.

Failed check:

```text
Stage 04 ACT row: delta_result token 'predicate-identity-separated' is outside controlled vocabulary for M9; allowed: category-separated, person-nature-transfer-blocked, predicate-separated, referent-separated, sense-separated
```

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage 04 normalization and
`tools/delta_result_vocabulary.py` / source vocabulary owner surface. The
case prompt is evidence, not the owner surface.

5 Whys:
1. Stage 04 failed because the ACT row used `predicate-identity-separated`.
2. The token failed because M9 controlled vocabulary allows
   `predicate-separated`, `referent-separated`, `sense-separated`,
   `category-separated`, and `person-nature-transfer-blocked`, not the new
   compound token.
3. The model invented a more specific phrase because the live predication
   pressure involved identity/person-language, but the Stage 04 prompt did not
   force choosing the nearest existing M9 owner-local token.
4. The no-model vocabulary canaries proved many owner-family tokens but did not
   include this identity/predication near-alias.
5. Recurrence is prevented by either adding a source-owned alias/canonical
   mapping to an existing M9 token if semantically equivalent, or expanding the
   source-owned M9 vocabulary only with a formal no-model canary proving the
   distinct delta.

Hansei:
Controlled deltas are doing their job. A model-authored near-synonym must not
be accepted as proof unless the source vocabulary or canonicalizer owns it.

Countermeasure:
Proposed next lane: no-model Stage 04 delta-vocabulary repair. Preferred first
Gemba is the source vocabulary and existing M9 canaries; do not loosen the
checker to pass arbitrary near-synonyms.

## Gate62C DSL/IR Transition Classification Addendum

Owner steer:
DSL/IR is the central release-relevant architectural invariant. The goal is
not merely "make the checker pass"; the goal is to keep the burden transition
typed, explicit, staged, and reconstructible:

```text
burden_id
-> route / TTP selection
-> owner operation
-> ACT row
-> controlled delta_result
-> MRP / NAR update
-> field_witness mirror
-> public output witness
-> graph / sidecar eligibility
```

Repair rule:
Do not repair by adding case-specific wording, broad aliases, or prose
tolerance. A countermeasure is not closed unless it names the DSL transition
protected and no-model canaries prove that transition.

Failure classification legend:
- `producer/scaffold`: the prompt/scaffold/model-facing producer failed to
  request or emit the typed DSL surface.
- `checker/normalizer`: the checker/normalizer failed to preserve a typed DSL
  surface or allowed ambiguity into canonical fields.
- `model semantic`: the model made a semantic error inside an otherwise valid
  DSL trace.
- `transport/infrastructure`: OS, model transport, Git, CI, or workspace
  infrastructure failed before meaningful DSL evidence existed.

| ID | failure class | protected DSL transition | why this classification is release-relevant | canary evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `GATE62B-STAGE01-CUSTODY-METADATA-STARVATION` | `producer/scaffold` | source boundary / custody -> Stage 01 intake record, while custody remains outside route/proof behavior | De-hardcoding removed route-gated metadata too aggressively and starved the intake DSL boundary. The fix restores custody without making case/path strings proof inputs. | Stage 01 prompt self-test redacts custody block and proves route-facing invariance outside it. |
| `GATE62B-STAGE03-RICH-BURDEN-FLOOR-NORMALIZATION` | `checker/normalizer` | `burden_id -> route / TTP selection` | Stage 02 labels in canonical `burden_floor` prevented exact comparison with Stage 03 route targets. ### Andon GATE62C-TST-STAGE04-ACT-ROW-DETAILS-STRING

Symptom:
The TST smoke passed Stage 01-03, then Stage 04 failed after the model placed
human prose strings in `act_row_details[]`.

Failed check:

```text
stage-04 act_row_details[0] must be an object
```

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, specifically Stage 04 prompt
wording and ACT-detail normalization. The TST prompt is failure evidence, not
the owner surface.

5 Whys:
1. Stage 04 failed because `act_row_details[0]` was a string.
2. It was a string because the model used the optional detail field as prose
   explanation instead of object-shaped handoff evidence.
3. The prompt says richer metadata may go in `act_row_details`, but does not
   state that `act_row_details[]` must be objects tied to `act_rows`.
4. Existing self-tests rejected object-shaped `act_rows` without `act_row`, but
   did not cover string-shaped `act_row_details`.
5. Recurrence is prevented by adding a no-model canary for string detail rows
   and by making the prompt/normalizer contract unambiguous: details are
   object rows or omitted; prose belongs outside canonical proof fields.

Hansei:
Optional detail fields are still proof-surface fields. If prose is allowed
there, the handoff can fail before MRP even sees the burden graph.

Countermeasure:
Proposed next lane: Stage 04 ACT detail schema hardening. Do not accept
unstructured prose as proof. Either normalize only when an `act_row` or
`body_ref` can be tied to the canonical ACT row, or reject with a clearer
prompt/canary pair.

Smoke B command/result:
Pending. No countermeasure applied yet. Proposed Smoke B:

```powershell

python tools\check_staged_runtime_handshake.py
```

Remaining blocker or closure:
Open. Blocks Stage 07, Stage 08, retained promotion, package, tag, release,
and provenance.

### Andon GATE62C-SECULARISM-STAGE04-BODY-REF-DIALECT-MISMATCH

Symptom:
The Secularism smoke passed Stage 01-03, then Stage 04 failed because
`act_row_details[]` could not be normalized against canonical ACT rows/body
refs.

Failed check:

```text
stage-04 act_row_details[0] cannot be normalized without a parseable act_row or body_ref tied to Stage 04 act_rows
```

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, Stage 04 ACT row/detail/body_ref
normalization.

5 Whys:
1. Stage 04 failed because detail objects omitted a direct parseable `body_ref`
   and the `owner_id` form did not let the normalizer tie the detail to an
   accepted ACT row.
2. The mismatch existed because the model used dotted body refs such as
   `B1.FPD.foreign-premise-detection` in `act_body_refs`, while ACT row heads
   used `B1[...]` and details only repeated `act_row`.
3. The prompt allows canonical burden IDs in ACT rows, but does not force one
   stable submove token dialect across `act_body_refs`, `body_ref=`, and
   details.
4. Existing canaries cover some body_ref mismatch cases, but not this
   neutral/secularism shape where dotted owner-family refs and ACT rows diverge.
5. Recurrence is prevented by a no-model canary requiring details to either
   carry a direct body_ref that appears in Stage 04 `act_rows`, or normalize
   from an exact `act_row` without ambiguity; the producer prompt should prefer
   the same body_ref token everywhere.

Hansei:
Body refs are the join key for public/witness reconstruction. Multiple
dialects inside the same stage make later NAR and Grapher parity fragile even
if the prose sounds coherent.

Countermeasure:
Proposed next lane: Stage 04 body_ref/detail dialect hardening. Patch the
owner surface generically; do not add secularism-specific logic.

   `delta_result` token for its `body_ref`.
4. Existing canaries cover Stage 06 delta-list values but did not prevent this

Stage 06 is the witness mirror, not a lossy summary. If it collapses
owner-local delta tokens into generic lists, proof reconstruction loses the
owner/delta/source-status obligation.

## Andon GATE62-MATRIX-STAGE01-CODEX-APPSTATE-BLOCKER

Symptom:
All four one-shot matrix runs failed before Stage 01 could receive a model
response. No `output.md` was produced for any case.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --case-name matrix-*-gate62 --raw-input[-path] <assigned input> --run-dir .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260605-023531-matrix-* --model gpt-5.5 --release-output-mode compiled --target-output-kb 100 --section-expansion-rounds 2 --transport-retry-rounds 2
```

First failed artifacts:

```text
.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-023531-matrix-trinitarian-j173/responses/stage-01-intake.codex-log.txt
.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-023531-matrix-tst-lillard/responses/stage-01-intake.codex-log.txt
.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-023531-matrix-khaybar/responses/stage-01-intake.codex-log.txt
.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-023531-matrix-secularism/responses/stage-01-intake.codex-log.txt
```

Owner file / owner surface:
External Codex model-transport/app-state surface, specifically the in-process
app-server client and `C:\Users\theis\.codex\state_5.sqlite` / temporary arg0
state path. Repo-local runner code is the reporting surface, not the failing
owner surface.

5 Whys:
1. The matrix produced no Stage 01 responses because each `codex exec`
   subprocess exited with code 1.
2. Each subprocess exited because the in-process app-server client failed to
   initialize with Windows `Access is denied. (os error 5)`.
3. The app-server client could not initialize because it could not clean stale
   arg0 temp dirs, could not update PATH through the arg0 path, and reported a
   readonly write failure against `C:\Users\theis\.codex\state_5.sqlite`.
4. The matrix launch allowed this to affect all four cases because all four
   isolated runners started while the same external Codex app-state path was
   unavailable for writes.
5. Recurrence is prevented by adding a matrix preflight/operating rule: before
   spending four model smokes, prove the Codex app-state/model-transport write
   path can launch at least one Stage 01 subprocess, or run a refreshed matrix
   only after the external app-state lock/permission condition is cleared.

Hansei:
Subagent isolation protected tracked files and smoke artifacts, but it did not
isolate the external Codex app-state database. A shared app-state failure is not
case behavior and must not be interpreted as Trinitarian, TST, Khaybar, or
Secularism proof evidence.

Countermeasure:
No repo source countermeasure was applied. The local no-model runner health was
verified with `run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test` and staged
handshake checks. The next safe action is external app-state repair or owner
confirmation that the Codex state path is writable/unlocked, followed by a
fresh matrix launch from new run directories. Do not rerun these failed case
directories or claim semantic evidence from them.

Smoke B command/result:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test
python tools\check_staged_runtime_handshake.py
```

Result:
PASS. This proves the repo-local staged runner and no-model handshake are
healthy; it does not prove model transport is restored.

```powershell
"Return GATE62_APPSTATE_PREFLIGHT_OK exactly." | codex exec -C <worktree> -s read-only -m gpt-5.5 -c 'approval_policy="never"' -c 'shell_environment_policy.inherit="all"' --output-last-message .daee\validation\20260605-gate62-appstate-preflight\output.txt -
```

signature. No preflight output file was produced.

The OS/app-state boundary was repaired without mutating the locked home
database by using a short workspace-local Codex home and temp path, copying the
existing local auth file without printing credential contents, clearing proxy
environment variables, and pointing TLS at Git's CA bundle:

```powershell
$env:REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=$env:SSL_CERT_FILE
"Return GATE62_APPSTATE_PREFLIGHT_OK exactly." | codex exec -C <fresh-checkout> -s read-only -m gpt-5.5 --disable plugins --ignore-user-config -c 'approval_policy="never"' -c 'shell_environment_policy.inherit="all"' --output-last-message .daee\validation\20260605-gate62-appstate-preflight-auth\output.txt -
```

PASS. The command returned `GATE62_APPSTATE_PREFLIGHT_OK`.

`C:\workspace\ai\daee-epistemics\_gate62_clean_checkout_20260605_0305` was
initialized and fetched from `origin/main`. It is clean, writable, and at
`4bfe0d9e8cfc6c8a68dc75a794b4fb0d397ced0f`.

8, retained promotion, package, tag, GitHub Release, release assets, public
sample publication, provenance, and release claims remain closed until a fresh
post-repair matrix produces case evidence.

### Andon GATE62-GIT-REMOTE-HTTPS-POPUP

Symptom:
The clean checkout could commit the Gate62 countermeasure record locally, but
`git push` failed twice with exit code `1` and no terminal stderr while the
user observed a popup application error.

Failed check:

```powershell
git -c http.proxy= -c https.proxy= push --porcelain origin HEAD:main
```

Owner file / owner surface:
Local Git for Windows HTTPS remote helper / host OS process surface. The
tracked docs file and GitHub remote were not the failing owner surfaces.

5 Whys:
1. The push did not update `origin/main` because the Git remote helper exited
   with code `1`.
2. The terminal did not include a Git diagnostic because the failure manifested
   as a Windows popup application error.
3. The failure persisted after clearing proxy variables, so it was not the
   earlier `127.0.0.1:9` proxy refusal.
4. This path was fragile because Git for Windows HTTPS execution depends on
   local helper process startup, while the repo docs lane only needed a
   one-file non-release update.
5. Recurrence is prevented by using the GitHub contents API for this docs-only
   ledger update when `git-remote-https` crashes, while preserving normal
   safety scans and CI/Pages monitoring.

Hansei:
A successful local commit is not a successful push. When the remote helper
crashes outside terminal reporting, the lane must switch to a verifiable API
write or stop, not silently assume GitHub received the commit.

Countermeasure:
Use the GitHub contents API to update the single tracked Markdown ledger file
on `main` after checks pass. Do not use this as package, tag, release, or
provenance evidence.

Smoke B command/result:
The first API attempt failed because the PowerShell string interpolated the
contents URL without bracing `${path}`, producing a nil blob SHA and a GitHub
422 response. Correct the URI construction before retrying.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Open until the API commit lands on `main` and CI/Pages pass.

### Andon GATE62-GIT-INDEX-STAGING-PERMISSION

Symptom:
The first attempt to stage the Gate62 Markdown ledger failed before commit.

Failed check:

```powershell
git add docs\audits\v0.4.4.0-postgate52-four-smoke-matrix.md
```

Failure:

```text
fatal: Unable to create 'C:/workspace/ai/daee-epistemics/expansion/v.4.3.0/work-v0.4.3.0/.git/worktrees/_tmp_stage07_v7_repair_20260603/index.lock': Permission denied
```

Owner file / owner surface:
Linked worktree Git metadata at
`C:\workspace\ai\daee-epistemics\expansion\v.4.3.0\work-v0.4.3.0\.git\worktrees\_tmp_stage07_v7_repair_20260603`.
The tracked audit doc is not the failing owner surface.

5 Whys:
1. The doc could not be staged because Git could not create `index.lock`.
2. Git needed `index.lock` because every index mutation writes through the
   linked-worktree gitdir rather than the visible worktree root.
3. The linked gitdir write was denied at the staging moment even though the
   worktree content path was writable.
4. The commit gate assumed the linked gitdir remained writable because prior
   commits in this worktree had succeeded and `git status` could read it.
5. Recurrence is prevented by inspecting the linked gitdir and stale lock state
   before retrying staging; if write denial repeats, stop as a Git metadata
   environment blocker rather than accumulating local-only docs.

Hansei:
Git read access is not Git write access. A clean worktree and writable source
file do not prove the linked worktree metadata can accept a commit.

Countermeasure:
Inspected `git rev-parse --git-dir`, the `.git` gitdir pointer, and the linked
gitdir contents. No stale `index.lock` existed after the failure. Retry staging
only after this custody record; the retry failed with the same permission
denial. The linked gitdir ACL also shows explicit DENY entries affecting write
permission, so commit/push cannot be forced from this sandbox.

Smoke B command/result:
FAIL: `git add docs\audits\v0.4.4.0-postgate52-four-smoke-matrix.md`
repeated the same `index.lock: Permission denied` failure.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Blocked. The Gate62 matrix Andon is recorded locally in Markdown and ignored
ActiveGraph custody, but it is not committed or pushed because the local
linked-worktree Git metadata is not writable from this environment.

### Andon GATE62B-STAGE01-CUSTODY-METADATA-STARVATION

Symptom:
Three smoke subagents reached model transport, but Stage 01 returned `fail`
because it could not honestly restate `case_id` and/or `retained_input`.

Failed check:

```powershell

```

First failed gate:
`stage-01-intake`.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, specifically `stage_prompt()` and
the Stage 01 intake contract. The failing owner surface is not Trinitarian,
Khaybar, or Secularism semantic content.

5 Whys:
1. Stage 01 failed because the model was asked to restate custody fields that
   were not present in the prompt.
2. The fields were not present because the hardcoding repair redacted
   case/path metadata from the model-facing route surface.
3. The redaction went too far because it treated custody metadata and
   route/proof behavior as the same risk surface.
4. The self-test only proved case/path prompt invariance, so it failed to
   prove that Stage 01 retained enough custody information to complete honestly.
5. Recurrence is prevented by adding a bounded custody-only prompt block and a
   no-model self-test that permits case/path values only inside that block and
   redacts the block before checking route-facing invariance.

Hansei:
De-hardcoding must not become de-custody. Case names and retained paths are
forbidden as proof/routing/canonicalization gates, but Stage 01 still needs
custody facts to avoid invented intake evidence.

Countermeasure:
`stage_prompt()` now emits a bounded "Custody metadata for Stage 01 only" JSON
block containing `case_id`, `retained_input`, and `input_digest`. The prompt
explicitly says these fields are not route evidence, owner-selection evidence,
proof eligibility, canonicalization input, or a case library key.

```powershell
python tools\check_staged_runtime_handshake.py
```

PASS after the countermeasure.

### Andon GATE62D-STAGE04-BODY-REF-LAYER-SEPARATION

Symptom:
All four model-smoke subagents reached Stage 04 and emitted ACT rows, but the
Stage 04 handoff failed before Stage 05 because the model put
`[owner.operation]` inside the `body_ref` field, for example
`body_ref=B1_1[M9.predication-repair]` and
`body_ref=¹B₁[source-status-repair.source-order]`.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --case-name matrix-*-gate62d --raw-input[-path] <assigned input> --run-dir .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260605-041337-matrix-* --model gpt-5.5 --release-output-mode compiled --target-output-kb 100 --section-expansion-rounds 2 --transport-retry-rounds 2
```

First failed gate:
`stage-04-burden-execution-act`, before Stage 05 MRP.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, Stage 04 scaffold and Stage 04
ACT/detail/body_ref checker surface.

Failure class:
`producer/scaffold failed to emit the DSL layer separation`.

Protected DSL transition:
`burden_id -> selected owner/TTP -> owner operation -> ACT row -> bare body_ref
join key -> register_axis -> controlled delta_result -> MRP/NAR handoff`.

5 Whys:
1. Stage 04 failed because the model emitted owner-qualified `body_ref`
   strings instead of bare burden/submove join keys.
2. It did that because the prior scaffold required the ACT head and
   `body_ref=` token to match, but did not distinguish public ACT notation
   from the typed join key.
3. The checker rejected the rows because its strict parser already treats
   owner/operation as a separate bracketed ACT slot and cannot tie polluted
   `body_ref` values to later MRP/NAR/field_witness keys.
4. The no-model canaries covered string details and head/body_ref dialect
   mismatch, but did not cover the equal-but-polluted case where the ACT head,
   `body_ref=`, `act_body_refs[]`, and details all repeated the same invalid
   owner-qualified token.
5. Recurrence is prevented by making Stage 04 layer separation explicit:
   `body_ref` is only the stable burden/submove join key; `owner_id`,
   `operation`, `register_axis`, and `delta_result` are separate typed
   fields, with owner/register and owner/delta checker-owned mappings.

Hansei:
The public ACT row is a rendered witness, but `body_ref` is a DSL join key.
Letting rendered owner notation leak into the join key collapses separate
typed layers and makes later public/witness/MRP/graph parity impossible to
reason about honestly.

Countermeasure:
The Stage 04 scaffold now says that the ACT head may be public
owner-qualified notation such as `¹B₁[source-status-repair.source-order]`, but
`body_ref=` and every `act_body_refs[]` item must be only the bare submove key
such as `¹B₁`. Stage 04 `act_row_details[]` is required and must carry
separate `body_ref`, `burden_id`, `owner_id`, `operation`, `register_axis`, and
`delta_result` evidence. The checker rejects brackets in `body_ref`, requires
detail `delta_result` to match the parsed ACT row and owner-local controlled
vocabulary, and validates a checker-owned owner/register-axis floor for
source-status repair, authority-order repair, and M9.

Smoke B command/result:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test
python - <<'PY'
# imported runner; replayed the four Gate62D Stage 04 response artifacts through
# normalized_stage('stage-04-burden-execution-act', ...)
PY
```

Result:
PASS. The self-test passes. The four actual Gate62D Stage 04 response artifacts
now fail as expected with:

```text
stage-04 ACT row body_ref must be the bare burden/submove join key; owner.operation belongs in ACT bracket/object fields, not body_ref
```

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed for no-model runner/scaffold hardening after Smoke B. It is not proof
that any matrix case passes Stage 04; a fresh post-repair matrix is required
after commit/push/CI green.

### Andon GATE62D-REPAIR-LANE-BODY-REF-CANONICALIZATION-LAUNDERING

Symptom:
The first repair-lane patch attempted to canonicalize redundant
owner-qualified `body_ref` forms into bare join keys. That would have made bad
model output checker-compliant by rewriting evidence after the fact.

Failed check:
Owner steer / DSL invariant review. The wrong countermeasure would have
treated `body_ref=B1_1[M9.predication-repair]` as a repairable dialect instead
of an invalid typed trace.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, the abandoned Stage 04 body_ref
canonicalization path.

Failure class:
`checker/normalizer failed to preserve the DSL` risk in the proposed
countermeasure, caught before commit.

Protected DSL transition:
`ACT row -> bare body_ref join key -> MRP/NAR/field_witness/public witness`.

5 Whys:
1. The proposed patch was wrong because it normalized polluted `body_ref`
   strings instead of rejecting them.
2. It normalized them because the immediate symptom looked like a parser
   dialect mismatch.
3. That framing was too shallow because it ignored the newly established
   DSL/IR layer-separation invariant: join key, owner/TTP, operation,
   register_axis, and delta_result are different typed fields.
4. The first no-model canary direction would have rewarded output that sounded
   structurally close while still mixing typed layers.
5. Recurrence is prevented by deleting the canonicalization path, making
   polluted `body_ref` a negative canary, and requiring separate
   `register_axis` plus owner-local `delta_result` evidence in Stage 04 details.

Hansei:
Canonicalizers can become evidence laundering when they repair model-authored
proof surfaces that should have been emitted as typed DSL in the first place.
The runner may normalize harmless aliases only when the typed transition is
preserved; it must not hide layer violations.

Countermeasure:
Removed the owner-qualified body_ref canonicalization direction. The checker
now rejects bracketed owner/operation text in `body_ref` fields and
`act_body_refs[]`; accepts bare `body_ref` only when detail rows carry separate
typed owner/operation/register/delta evidence; and adds canaries for source
status on `σ`, M9 predication/meaning-density on approved M9 axes, and
owner-qualified body_ref rejection.

Smoke B command/result:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test
```

Result:
PASS. Additional negative replay of all four Gate62D Stage 04 artifacts PASSed
by failing for the bare-join-key invariant.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed as a repair-lane process Andon. No model smoke rerun has occurred.

### Gate62D Owner Steer Addendum: Public Projection Invariant

Registered after the Stage 04 layer-separation repair and before the next
model-smoke matrix.

Public `output.md` is a human-facing projection of the DSL/IR trace, not merely
a polished final answer. Machine-only details such as raw JSON, attempt
metadata, full register maps, hash records, and verifier sidecar payloads may
remain outside the public output, but the public burden/submove scaffold must
not be compressed away.

Required public projection surfaces:

- NOETIC FIELD EXECUTION / initial field header.
- Layer A compact DSL/IR header.
- Live burden list and held burden list.
- Source-status / noetic-frame / gate-release decision.
- Visible burden sections and visible submoves per burden.
- Target / Operation / Result for each public submove.
- Land(Bn) or HOLD/PARTIAL/RECURSE status for each burden.
- R(H,Delta) handoff after each burden.
- Field diagnostics where required.
- Closure/Reconstruction Witness.
- Final Restorative Response.
- Closing Formulation.

Protected DSL transition:
`ACT row -> controlled delta_result -> MRP/NAR update -> field_witness mirror
-> public output witness -> graph/sidecar eligibility`.

Anti-slimming rule:
Removing machine-only noise is allowed. Removing public noetic execution is
not. Any post-formalism reduction in accepted burden count, generated-burden
count, ACT/submove count, NAR/field_witness coverage, or public-output witness
coverage remains an Andon unless Gemba proves the removed material was
duplicate, invalid, inert, source-blocked, held, unsupported, or collapsed into
a stronger typed transition.

Expanded projection guard:
The staged artifacts and sidecars may contain the full machine DSL/IR trace,
but the public output cannot become a detached summary while the "real
structure" lives only in sidecars. The model must reason over the full staged
DSL/IR state before producing the public projection. The public projection must
render the whole structure human-readably enough that a governed reader can see
the noetic work, while machine-only tables, attempt metadata, hashes, raw JSON
dumps, and checker-only payloads may remain in staged artifacts or sidecars.

Skill-as-code staging must strengthen whole-field synthesis, not
compartmentalize it. Each stage must carry forward the full live burden state,
held burdens, generated burdens, submoves, owner/TTP activations, MRP/NAR
updates, field_witness mirrors, and public-output obligations into the next
stage.

A staged repair is a regression if it makes output easier to validate by
weakening any of:

- burden visibility;
- submove visibility;
- Layer A reading;
- burden dependency collapse;
- R(H,Delta) handoffs;
- Closure/Reconstruction Witness;
- Final Restorative Response;
- Closing Formulation;
- whole noetic-field synthesis.

No-model canaries required before claiming this surface closed:

- `output.md` retains the public governed scaffold.
- `output.md` includes visible burden and submove execution, not merely final
  prose.
- Sidecar-only machine details may be omitted only when the public answer still
  mirrors the full burden graph.
- A slim final answer without Layer A, burden sections, Land(Bn), R(H,Delta),
  and Closure/Reconstruction Witness is rejected.

Correct target:

```text
full machine DSL/IR trace in staged artifacts
-> visible public burden/submove execution in output.md
-> clean final restorative prose after the proof scaffold
```

If the final output becomes cleaner but less whole, less connected, less
burden-aware, or less restorative, treat that as an Andon.

### Gate62E No-Model Canary Hardening: DSL Layering And Public Projection

Status:
Implemented as a no-model checker/fixture hardening lane before the next model
matrix.

Gemba:
The Stage 04 runner already rejected owner-qualified `body_ref` values and
required typed `act_row_details`. The remaining risk was narrower canary
coverage: M9 had approved `μ`, `ξ`, and `Ω` register axes in code, but the
self-test did not explicitly prove the residue/slippage `ξ` branch or reject
cross-owner register-axis swaps. The manual render contract required the public
scaffold, but there was no explicit slim sidecar-only invalid fixture.

Protected DSL transitions:

- `burden_id -> route/TTP selection -> owner operation -> ACT row`.
- `ACT row -> bare body_ref join key -> typed owner_id/operation/register_axis`.
- `controlled delta_result -> MRP/NAR update -> field_witness mirror`.
- `field_witness mirror -> public output witness`.

Countermeasures:

- Added Stage 04 no-model canaries proving M9 residue/slippage may bind to
  `ξ` through a separate `register_axis` field, while `σ` is rejected for M9.
- Added Stage 04 no-model canaries proving source-status repair remains bound
  to `σ`, while `μ` is rejected for source-status repair.
- Added `tests/manual-smoke-render/invalid/slim-sidecar-only-public-projection.md`
  to prove a polished public answer with sidecar-ish machine structure but no
  Layer A, Layer B, visible burden/submove execution, Land, R(H,Delta), or
  Closure/Reconstruction Witness remains invalid.

Anti-slimming / prose-dependence note:
This hardening does not lower burden, submove, ACT, MRP/NAR, field_witness, or
public-witness coverage. It prevents two failure modes: treating prose or
sidecar-only state as the proof substrate, and letting register semantics leak
into `body_ref` or owner-operation text. The DSL/IR state remains the execution
substrate; prose remains the human-facing projection and restorative delivery.

Smoke B command/result:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test
python tools\check_manual_smoke_render_contract.py --skip-skill-contract
python tools\build_staged_governed_output.py --self-test
```

Result:
PASS. The staged runner self-test passed, the manual render contract passed
with 4 valid fixtures and 27 invalid fixtures checked, and the staged governed
output assembly self-test passed.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed locally for no-model canary hardening after Smoke B. Commit/push and
CI/Pages green remain required before any new model-smoke matrix.

### Andon GATE62E-CACHED-SECRET-SCAN-COMMAND-FAILURE

Symptom:
The first cached secret-scan PowerShell command failed with
`Value cannot be null. (Parameter 'input')`.

Failed check:
Cached diff secret-scan command.

Owner file / owner surface:
IMPLEMENTAUDIT shell/check invocation surface for safety scans. No source file
owned the failure.

Failure class:
`transport/infrastructure failure` / process command failure. The failure did
not indicate a secret; it indicated that the scan command did not robustly
handle empty/null diff capture.

5 Whys:
1. The cached secret scan failed because `[regex]::IsMatch()` received `$null`
   for its input.
2. `$diff` was `$null` because the PowerShell command captured the git diff
   call through a fragile array/path invocation.
3. The command was fragile because it mixed an explicit path array with shell
   command substitution rather than scanning the cached diff as one string.
4. This was not caught earlier because prior scans used unstaged `git diff`
   output, not this exact staged-array variant.
5. Recurrence is prevented by using `(git diff --cached) -join "`n"` and
   explicitly converting null to an empty string before regex evaluation.

Hansei:
Safety scans must fail only on real evidence or a clearly named command
blocker. Fragile scan wrappers create noise and can obscure the actual gate.

Countermeasure:
Rerun the cached secret scan with a null-safe staged-diff capture, then require
PASS before commit.

Smoke B command/result:

```powershell
$diffLines = git diff --cached
$diff = ($diffLines -join "`n")
# then run the existing secret regex against the null-safe string
```

Result:
PASS. The null-safe cached secret scan returned `CACHED_SECRET_SCAN_PASS`.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed. The staged safety scan command failure was corrected and did not reveal
secret material.

### Andon GATE62E-TST-DO-SECOND-LOOP-DELTA-OWNER-BLOCKER

Symptom:
The TST run passed Stages 01-03 but stopped at Stage 04. The Stage 04 model
response explicitly refused to emit ACT rows because `do-second-loop` was
routed for `B2`, `B3`, and `B4`, while no owner-local controlled
`delta_result` tokens were available for that owner family.

Failed check:

```powershell
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --case-name matrix-tst-lillard-gate62e --raw-input-path tests\smokes\v0.4.3.0-release-regression\prompts\02-tst-lillard.md --run-dir .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260605-052115-matrix-tst-lillard --model gpt-5.5 --release-output-mode compiled --target-output-kb 100 --section-expansion-rounds 2 --transport-retry-rounds 2
```

First failed gate:
`stage-04-burden-execution-act`.

Exact artifact/path:
`.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-052115-matrix-tst-lillard/responses/stage-04-burden-execution-act.response.txt`
and
`.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-052115-matrix-tst-lillard/records/staged-handoff-failure.json`.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, Stage 03/04 owner-route to callable
ACT/delta floor. The likely owner-source gap is the controlled operation and
`delta_result` mapping for `do-second-loop`.

Failure class:
`producer/scaffold failed to emit the DSL` because the route was selected but
no executable owner-local ACT transition was available.

Protected DSL transition:
`burden_id -> route/TTP selection -> owner operation -> ACT row -> controlled
delta_result`.

5 Whys:

1. Stage 04 failed because `act_burdens` was empty.
2. `act_burdens` was empty because the model refused to emit `do-second-loop`
   ACT rows without owner-local controlled `delta_result` tokens.
3. The owner route existed because Stage 03 correctly selected
   `do-second-loop` for accountability, guidance, and punishment-proportionality
   pressures.
4. The scaffold/checker contract allowed route selection to outrun the
   callable owner-operation/delta vocabulary floor for that owner family.
5. Recurrence is prevented by adding a no-model owner-family canary and mapping
   for `do-second-loop` operations so selected routes either execute with
   controlled deltas or HOLD/PARTIAL explicitly without fake Land.

Hansei:
This is exactly the DSL/IR availability-versus-activation boundary. A selected
route is not executable proof until it has a callable owner operation and
controlled burden-state delta.

Countermeasure:
Proposed next no-model repair: add or align `do-second-loop` operation and
delta_result vocabulary for the routed accountability/hujjah, coercive-guidance,
and punishment-proportionality branches; add positive availability and safe
HOLD/PARTIAL canaries. Do not borrow M8, P7, SOURCE, or M9 tokens.

Smoke B command/result:
No countermeasure applied yet. The matrix artifact is preserved as failure
evidence. Smoke B will be the no-model Stage 03/04 owner-delta canary suite
plus the runner self-test after patching.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Open. Blocks TST Stage 04, Stage 07, Stage 08, retained promotion, release
matrix closure, and release.

and

were promoted into the callable operation slot.

`route/TTP selection -> owner operation -> ACT row -> controlled delta_result
-> MRP/NAR update -> public output witness -> graph/sidecar eligibility`.

   operation for `source-status-repair`; the registered options are `sort` and

   as an operation floor instead of as pressure/result evidence under a
   source-order operation.

   delta/result or route-pressure label.

   not the Trinitarian hidden-support source-status route shape.
5. Recurrence is prevented by making hidden-support a pressure/result label
   under a registered source-status operation and adding a neutral canary for
   source-status unregistered-operation rejection.

Hansei:
The output was long and structurally rich, but a single operation-token drift
invalidated the proof chain. Public body richness cannot compensate for a typed
owner-operation mismatch.

Countermeasure:
Proposed next no-model repair: patch the route/scaffold so source-status
hidden-support pressure emits `source-status-repair.source-order` or another
registered source-owned operation, with `proof-text-hidden-support-blocked` in

owner decision expands the controlled mapping with canaries.

### Andon GATE62F-IR-RECONSTRUCTION-SELECTED-FPD-MODULE-GAP

Symptom:
Gate62F Smoke B failed `python tools\check_ir_reconstruction_fixtures.py` on
`imported-tribunal-protest`.

Failed check:
`imported-tribunal-protest: selected catalogue owner foreign-premise-detection
not present in matched_modules`.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tests/reconstruction-fixtures/required-contrast-and-stability.json`.

5 Whys:

1. Why did the checker fail? The fixture selected `foreign-premise-detection`
   but omitted it from `candidate_ir.matched_modules`.
2. Why was the selected owner omitted? The fixture relied on
   `foreign_premise` / `upstream_findings` prose fields as evidence for FPD.
3. Why was prose evidence insufficient? `foreign-premise-detection` is a
   catalogue owner, so active selection must carry a loaded module identity.
4. Why did this surface during Gate62F? The lane tightened typed owner
   operation discipline and reran reconstruction parity, exposing a stale
   fixture dialect that allowed selected-owner/proof-load asymmetry.
5. Why is the root cause important? It is the same DSL failure family as the
   model-smoke Andons: active owner work cannot be proved by prose or
   adjacent fields when the typed module/load surface is missing.

Hansei:
A fixture that accepts selected owner work without `matched_modules` teaches
the wrong lesson to the pipeline: route selection may appear reconstructible
even when the callable owner module is not part of the IR trace.

Countermeasure:
Added `foreign-premise-detection` to the fixture's `matched_modules` and added
its module entry to `source_basis`. Did not relax
`check_ir_reconstruction_fixtures.py`.

Smoke B command/result:

```powershell
python tools\check_ir_reconstruction_fixtures.py
```

Result: PASS. Six reconstruction fixtures and one stability group checked.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Closed for no-model fixture coverage. Does not itself prove a fresh model
smoke.

when the main patch is checker/runtime oriented, because release readiness
depends on truthful generated docs.

### Recovered Coordinator Andon GATE62F-KHAYBAR-CWD-DISPATCH

The first Khaybar subagent attempted to run the assigned smoke from
